\u003ccode\u003eunsloth studio\u003c/code\u003e binds to \u003ccode\u003e127.0.0.1\u003c/code\u003e (this machine only). To reach it from another device, pick one of:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--secure\u003c/code\u003e (recommended): serve \u003cstrong\u003eonly\u003c/strong\u003e through a free Cloudflare HTTPS link. Unsloth stays bound to localhost and the tunnel provides the public URL; it fails closed (does not start) if the tunnel can't come up, so the raw port is never exposed.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"unsloth studio --secure -p 8888\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003eunsloth studio --secure -p 8888\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-H 0.0.0.0\u003c/code\u003e: bind the raw port on all network interfaces, reachable from anywhere on the network (subject to your firewall). It does not create a public internet URL; add \u003ccode\u003e--cloudflare\u003c/code\u003e to also publish an internet-reachable \u003ccode\u003ehttps://*.trycloudflare.com\u003c/code\u003e link even behind a firewall. Only use this on a network you trust.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"unsloth studio -H 0.0.0.0 -p 8888\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003eunsloth studio -H 0.0.0.0 -p 8888\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe Cloudflare tunnel is \u003cstrong\u003eoff by default\u003c/strong\u003e: \u003ccode\u003e-H 0.0.0.0\u003c/code\u003e exposes the raw port only, not a public internet URL. Pair the wildcard bind with \u003ccode\u003e--cloudflare\u003c/code\u003e (\u003ccode\u003eunsloth studio -H 0.0.0.0 --cloudflare\u003c/code\u003e) to also publish a public \u003ccode\u003ehttps://*.trycloudflare.com\u003c/code\u003e link, or prefer \u003ccode\u003e--secure\u003c/code\u003e (above), which keeps the raw port private. \u003ccode\u003e--cloudflare\u003c/code\u003e has no effect on a loopback bind.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOn a wildcard bind Unsloth works out the address to share by asking \u003ccode\u003eifconfig.me\u003c/code\u003e for the public IP, then asks \u003ccode\u003echeck-host.net\u003c/code\u003e whether that port is reachable so it can tell you if a firewall is in the way. Both contact a third party. Set \u003ccode\u003eUNSLOTH_STUDIO_DISABLE_PUBLIC_CHECK=1\u003c/code\u003e to skip them; the banner then shows the LAN address and no reachability line.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe first time Unsloth is published on a public URL (\u003ccode\u003e--secure\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e--cloudflare\u003c/code\u003e) with the auto-generated admin password still in place, it asks for a new admin password in the terminal (masked input with confirmation) before the public link goes up. Without an attached terminal it warns instead and keeps the bootstrap deadline: Unsloth shuts down after \u003ccode\u003eUNSLOTH_STUDIO_BOOTSTRAP_TIMEOUT\u003c/code\u003e (default 1 hour) unless the password is changed in the web UI.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor headless setups that cannot answer that prompt, set the initial admin password non-interactively with \u003ccode\u003e--password\u003c/code\u003e (only takes effect when no password is set yet; if one already exists it is a hard error, so rotate later with \u003ccode\u003eunsloth studio reset-password\u003c/code\u003e):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"unsloth studio --secure --password 'your-strong-password'        # visible in `ps`/history\nUNSLOTH_STUDIO_PASSWORD='your-strong-password' unsloth studio --secure   # via env var\nprintf '%s\\n' 'your-strong-password' | unsloth studio --secure --password - Unsloth Studio lets you run and train models locally.

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">⭐ Features</h2><a id="user-content--features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ⭐ Features" href="#-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Unsloth Studio (Beta) lets you run and train text, <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/text-to-speech-tts-fine-tuning" rel="nofollow">audio</a>, <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/embedding-finetuning" rel="nofollow">embedding</a>, <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/vision-fine-tuning" rel="nofollow">vision</a> models on Windows, Linux and macOS.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Inference</h3><a id="user-content-inference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Inference" href="#inference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Search + download + run models</strong> including GGUF, LoRA adapters, safetensors</li>
<li><strong>Export models</strong>: <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/studio/export" rel="nofollow">Save or export</a> models to GGUF, 16-bit safetensors and other formats.</li>
<li><strong>Tool calling</strong>: Support for <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/studio/chat#auto-healing-tool-calling" rel="nofollow">self-healing tool calling</a> and web search</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/studio/chat#code-execution" rel="nofollow">Code execution</a></strong>: lets LLMs test code in Claude artifacts and sandbox environments</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/api" rel="nofollow">API inference endpoint</a></strong>: Deploy and run local LLMs in Claude Code, Codex tools with Unsloth</li>
<li><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/studio/chat#auto-parameter-tuning" rel="nofollow">Auto set inference settings</a> and customize chat templates.</li>
<li>We work directly with teams behind <a href="https://docs.unsloth.ai/new/gpt-oss-how-to-run-and-fine-tune#unsloth-fixes-for-gpt-oss" rel="nofollow">gpt-oss</a>, <a href="https://www.reddit.com/r/LocalLLaMA/comments/1kaodxu/qwen3_unsloth_dynamic_ggufs_128k_context_bug_fixes/" rel="nofollow">Qwen3</a>, <a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/12889" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/12889/hovercard">Llama 4</a>, <a href="https://huggingface.co/mistralai/Mistral-Medium-3.5-128B/discussions/18" rel="nofollow">Mistral</a>, <a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=39671146" rel="nofollow">Gemma 1-3</a>, and <a href="https://unsloth.ai/blog/phi4" rel="nofollow">Phi-4</a>, where we’ve fixed bugs that improve model accuracy.</li>
<li>Chat with images, audio, PDFs, code, DOCX and more. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/integrations/connections" rel="nofollow">Connect API providers</a> (OpenAI, Anthropic) or servers (vLLM, Ollama).</li>
<li><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/studio/chat#model-arena" rel="nofollow"><strong>Compare any two models</strong></a> side by side with the same prompt.</li>
<li><strong>OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible APIs</strong>: Serve local models through <code>/v1/chat/completions</code>, <code>/v1/responses</code> and <code>/v1/messages</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Connect local models to agents</strong>: Use <code>unsloth start</code> with Claude Code, Codex, Hermes and more.</li>
<li><strong>Web/PDF search</strong> can read PDF papers, manuals and other PDF results.</li>
<li><strong>GGUF hardware controls</strong>: Choose GPUs/layers, offload MoE experts, use multi-GPU or Tensor Parallelism.</li>
<li>The opt-in <strong>MCP control endpoint</strong> lets AI clients manage models, training, recipes and exports.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Training</h3><a id="user-content-training" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Training" href="#training"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Train and RL <strong>500+ models</strong> up to <strong>2x faster</strong> with <strong>70% less VRAM</strong>; MoE up to <strong>12x faster</strong>.</li>
<li>Train and run RL on <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/amd" rel="nofollow">AMD GPUs</a> across Windows, WSL and Linux.</li>
<li><strong>Data Recipes</strong>: <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/studio/data-recipe" rel="nofollow">Auto-create datasets</a> from <strong>PDF, CSV, DOCX</strong> etc. Edit data in a visual-node workflow.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/get-started/reinforcement-learning-rl-guide" rel="nofollow">Reinforcement Learning</a></strong> uses <strong>80% less VRAM</strong> for GRPO, FP8 and vision RL, with 7x longer contexts.</li>
<li><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/3x-faster-training-packing" rel="nofollow"><strong>Long-context training</strong></a>: <strong>3x faster</strong>, 30% less VRAM and 500K+ context.</li>
<li>Supports LoRA/QLoRA, full fine-tuning, RL, pretraining, 4-bit, 16-bit and FP8.</li>
<li>Custom Triton and mathematical <strong>kernels</strong> built with PyTorch and Hugging Face.</li>
<li><strong>Observability</strong>: Monitor training live, track loss and GPU usage and customize graphs.</li>
<li><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/multi-gpu-training-with-unsloth" rel="nofollow">Multi-GPU</a> training is supported, with major improvements coming soon.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚀 Unsloth Start</h2><a id="user-content--unsloth-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚀 Unsloth Start" href="#-unsloth-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/integrations/unsloth-start" rel="nofollow">Unsloth Start</a> connects <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/claude-code" rel="nofollow">Claude Code</a>, <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/codex" rel="nofollow">Codex</a> and other agents to local models with one command.</p>
<p dir="auto">Start Unsloth, load a model, open your project folder, then run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="unsloth start claude"><pre>unsloth start claude</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Replace <code>claude</code> with any supported agent:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Agent</th>
<th>Command</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Claude Code</td>
<td><code>unsloth start claude</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>OpenAI Codex</td>
<td><code>unsloth start codex</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Hermes Agent</td>
<td><code>unsloth start hermes</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>OpenClaw</td>
<td><code>unsloth start openclaw</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>OpenCode</td>
<td><code>unsloth start opencode</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Pi Coding Agent</td>
<td><code>unsloth start pi</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode and Pi can keep their current model and use Unsloth as a local
subagent:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="unsloth start claude --as-subagent --model unsloth/model-GGUF:quant"><pre>unsloth start claude --as-subagent --model unsloth/model-GGUF:quant</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📥 Install</h2><a id="user-content--install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📥 Install" href="#-install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Unsloth can be used in two ways: through <strong><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/studio/" rel="nofollow">Unsloth Studio</a></strong>, the web UI, or through <strong>Unsloth Core</strong>, the code-based version. Each has different requirements.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Unsloth Studio (web UI)</h3><a id="user-content-unsloth-studio-web-ui" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Unsloth Studio (web UI)" href="#unsloth-studio-web-ui"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Unsloth Studio (Beta) works on <strong>Windows, Linux, WSL</strong> and <strong>macOS</strong>.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>CPU:</strong> Supported for Chat and Data Recipes currently</li>
<li><strong>NVIDIA:</strong> Training works on RTX 30/40/50, Blackwell, DGX Spark, Station and more</li>
<li><strong>macOS:</strong> Training, MLX and GGUF inference are ALL supported.</li>
<li><strong>AMD:</strong> Training, RL, chat and deployment work on Windows, WSL and Linux. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/amd" rel="nofollow">Read the AMD guide</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Vulkan:</strong> GGUF inference is supported on <a href="https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth/pull/5819" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/unslothai/unsloth/pull/5819/hovercard">compatible GPUs, including Intel GPUs</a>. Vulkan accelerates GGUF inference only; training still requires a supported PyTorch or MLX backend.</li>
<li><strong>Multi-GPU:</strong> Available now, with a major upgrade on the way</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">macOS, Linux, WSL:</h4><a id="user-content-macos-linux-wsl-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: macOS, Linux, WSL:" href="#macos-linux-wsl-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh | sh"><pre>curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> sh</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Use the same command to update.</p>
<p dir="auto">To force the Vulkan llama.cpp backend, set <code>UNSLOTH_FORCE_VULKAN=1</code> <strong>before installing or updating</strong>. The setting selects the llama.cpp binary bundle, so setting it only when launching Studio cannot replace an existing CPU bundle:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="export UNSLOTH_FORCE_VULKAN=1
curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh | sh"><pre><span class="pl-k">export</span> UNSLOTH_FORCE_VULKAN=1
curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> sh</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Windows:</h4><a id="user-content-windows-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Windows:" href="#windows-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="irm https://unsloth.ai/install.ps1 | iex"><pre>irm https:<span class="pl-k">//</span>unsloth.ai<span class="pl-k">/</span>install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">|</span> iex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Use the same command to update.</p>
<p dir="auto">To force the Vulkan llama.cpp backend, set the environment variable before running the installer or updater:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$env:UNSLOTH_FORCE_VULKAN=1
irm https://unsloth.ai/install.ps1 | iex"><pre><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>UNSLOTH_FORCE_VULKAN</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>
irm https:<span class="pl-k">//</span>unsloth.ai<span class="pl-k">/</span>install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">|</span> iex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Re-running the current installer replaces a previously selected CPU bundle when the backend differs. A separate Vulkan SDK is not required; the GPU driver must provide a working Vulkan runtime.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Launch</h4><a id="user-content-launch" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Launch" href="#launch"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="unsloth studio -p 8888"><pre>unsloth studio -p 8888</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For LAN or cloud access, add <code>-H 0.0.0.0</code> (raw port only; add <code>--cloudflare</code> for a public URL). By default, Unsloth is accessible only locally.</p>
<p dir="auto">To reach Unsloth over HTTPS, use <code>unsloth studio --secure</code>. Unsloth stays bound to localhost and is reached only through a free Cloudflare tunnel, which publishes it at a public <code>https://*.trycloudflare.com</code> URL (it fails closed if the tunnel can't start, so the raw port is never exposed). This makes Unsloth reachable from the internet, so anyone with the link and API key can use it and run code: keep your API key private (see Remote access below).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Docker</h4><a id="user-content-docker" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Docker" href="#docker"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use our <a href="https://hub.docker.com/r/unsloth/unsloth" rel="nofollow">Docker image</a> <code>unsloth/unsloth</code> container. Run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d -e JUPYTER_PASSWORD=&quot;mypassword&quot; \
  -p 8888:8888 -p 8000:8000 -p 2222:22 \
  -v $(pwd)/work:/workspace/work \
  --gpus all \
  unsloth/unsloth"><pre>docker run -d -e JUPYTER_PASSWORD=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>mypassword<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \
  -p 8888:8888 -p 8000:8000 -p 2222:22 \
  -v <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>pwd<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span>/work:/workspace/work \
  --gpus all \
  unsloth/unsloth</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Developer, Nightly, Uninstall</h4><a id="user-content-developer-nightly-uninstall" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Developer, Nightly, Uninstall" href="#developer-nightly-uninstall"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To see developer, nightly and uninstallation etc. instructions, see <a href="#-advanced-installation">advanced installation</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Unsloth Core (code-based)</h3><a id="user-content-unsloth-core-code-based" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Unsloth Core (code-based)" href="#unsloth-core-code-based"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

You can use the same Docker image as Unsloth Studio.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">AMD, Intel:</h4><a id="user-content-amd-intel" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: AMD, Intel:" href="#amd-intel"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For RTX 50x, B200, 6000 GPUs: <code>uv pip install unsloth --torch-backend=auto</code>. Read our guides for: <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/blog/fine-tuning-llms-with-blackwell-rtx-50-series-and-unsloth" rel="nofollow">Blackwell</a> and <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/blog/fine-tuning-llms-with-nvidia-dgx-spark-and-unsloth" rel="nofollow">DGX Spark</a>. <br>
To install Unsloth on <strong>AMD</strong> and <strong>Intel</strong> GPUs, follow our <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/amd" rel="nofollow">AMD Guide</a> and <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/get-started/install/intel" rel="nofollow">Intel Guide</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📒 Free Notebooks</h2><a id="user-content--free-notebooks" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📒 Free Notebooks" href="#-free-notebooks"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🦥 Unsloth News</h2><a id="user-content--unsloth-news" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🦥 Unsloth News" href="#-unsloth-news"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>AMD training</strong>: Train, run RL, chat and deploy on AMD GPUs across Windows, WSL and Linux. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/amd" rel="nofollow">Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>GGUF hardware controls</strong>: Choose GPU/layer placement, offload MoE experts and use multi-GPU or Tensor Parallelism. <a href="https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth/pull/6414" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/unslothai/unsloth/pull/6414/hovercard">#6414</a></li>
<li><strong>Local models for any agent</strong>: Use <code>unsloth start</code> with Claude Code, Codex, Hermes, OpenCode, OpenClaw, Pi and more through Unsloth's OpenAI- and Anthropic-compatible APIs. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/api" rel="nofollow">Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>MCP control endpoint</strong>: Let compatible clients manage models, training, recipes, checkpoints and exports. <a href="https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth/pull/7191" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/unslothai/unsloth/pull/7191/hovercard">#7191</a></li>
<li><strong>Local inference reliability</strong>: Resume long chats faster, recover stalled downloads and reuse existing GGUF files. <a href="https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth/pull/7204" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/unslothai/unsloth/pull/7204/hovercard">#7204</a> • <a href="https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth/pull/6858" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/unslothai/unsloth/pull/6858/hovercard">#6858</a> • <a href="https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth/pull/7209" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/unslothai/unsloth/pull/7209/hovercard">#7209</a></li>
<li><strong>New models</strong>: <a href="https://huggingface.co/unsloth/Qwen-AgentWorld-35B-A3B-GGUF" rel="nofollow">Qwen-AgentWorld</a>, <a href="https://huggingface.co/unsloth/models?search=ornith" rel="nofollow">Ornith</a>, <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/kimi-k2.7-code" rel="nofollow">Kimi K2.7 Code</a> and <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/minimax-m3" rel="nofollow">MiniMax M3</a></li>
<li><strong>GLM-5.2</strong>: Run Z.ai's 744B-parameter, 1M-context open model locally with Unsloth Dynamic GGUFs. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/glm-5.2" rel="nofollow">Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4</strong>: Run DeepSeek-V4-Flash locally with corrected multi-turn and tool-calling behavior. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/deepseek-v4" rel="nofollow">Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>DiffusionGemma</strong>: Run and fine-tune Google's diffusion language model with 1.8x faster inference in Unsloth Studio. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/diffusiongemma" rel="nofollow">Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>Qwen3.6</strong>: Run and train Qwen3.6 with MTP for 1.4-2.2x faster inference and NVFP4 quants for supported GPUs. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/qwen3.6" rel="nofollow">Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>Gemma 4</strong>: Run and train Gemma 4 text, image and audio models with QAT, MTP, GGUF and MLX support. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/gemma-4" rel="nofollow">Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>MCP servers</strong>: Connect local models to files, apps, databases and external tools through Model Context Protocol. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/mcp" rel="nofollow">Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>Connections</strong>: Mix local models with API providers (OpenAI, Anthropic) or servers (vLLM, Ollama) in the same interface. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/integrations/connections" rel="nofollow">Guide</a></li>
<li><strong>Introducing Unsloth Studio</strong>: our new web UI for running and training LLMs. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/studio" rel="nofollow">Blog</a></li>
<li>Train <strong>MoE LLMs 12x faster</strong> with 35% less VRAM - DeepSeek, GLM, Qwen and gpt-oss. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/faster-moe" rel="nofollow">Blog</a></li>
<li><strong>Embedding models</strong>: Unsloth now supports ~1.8-3.3x faster embedding fine-tuning. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/embedding-finetuning" rel="nofollow">Blog</a> • <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/get-started/unsloth-notebooks#embedding-models" rel="nofollow">Notebooks</a></li>
<li>New <strong>7x longer context RL</strong> vs. all other setups, via our new batching algorithms. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/grpo-long-context" rel="nofollow">Blog</a></li>
<li>New RoPE &amp; MLP <strong>Triton Kernels</strong> &amp; <strong>Padding Free + Packing</strong>: 3x faster training &amp; 30% less VRAM. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/3x-faster-training-packing" rel="nofollow">Blog</a></li>
<li><strong>500K Context</strong>: Training a 20B model with &gt;500K context is now possible on an 80GB GPU. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/blog/500k-context-length-fine-tuning" rel="nofollow">Blog</a></li>
<li><strong>FP8 &amp; Vision RL</strong>: You can now do FP8 &amp; VLM GRPO on consumer GPUs. <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/get-started/reinforcement-learning-rl-guide/fp8-reinforcement-learning" rel="nofollow">FP8 Blog</a> • <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/get-started/reinforcement-learning-rl-guide/vision-reinforcement-learning-vlm-rl" rel="nofollow">Vision RL</a></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📥 Advanced Installation</h2><a id="user-content--advanced-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📥 Advanced Installation" href="#-advanced-installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The below advanced instructions are for Unsloth Studio. For Unsloth Core advanced installation, <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/get-started/install/pip-install#advanced-pip-installation" rel="nofollow">view our docs</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Developer / Nightly / Experimental installs: macOS, Linux, WSL:</h4><a id="user-content-developer--nightly--experimental-installs-macos-linux-wsl" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Developer / Nightly / Experimental installs: macOS, Linux, WSL:" href="#developer--nightly--experimental-installs-macos-linux-wsl"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The developer install builds from the <code>main</code> branch, which is the latest (nightly) source.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth
cd unsloth
./install.sh --local
unsloth studio -p 8888"><pre>git clone https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> unsloth
./install.sh --local
unsloth studio -p 8888</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">To install into an isolated location (its own virtual env, <code>auth/</code>, <code>studio.db</code>, cache and llama.cpp build), set <code>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME</code> and pass it again at launch:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME=&quot;$PWD/.studio&quot; ./install.sh --local
UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME=&quot;$PWD/.studio&quot; unsloth studio -p 8888"><pre>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$PWD</span>/.studio<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> ./install.sh --local
UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$PWD</span>/.studio<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> unsloth studio -p 8888</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then to update :</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cd unsloth &amp;&amp; git pull
./install.sh --local
unsloth studio -p 8888"><pre><span class="pl-c1">cd</span> unsloth <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> git pull
./install.sh --local
unsloth studio -p 8888</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Developer / Nightly / Experimental installs: Windows PowerShell:</h4><a id="user-content-developer--nightly--experimental-installs-windows-powershell" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Developer / Nightly / Experimental installs: Windows PowerShell:" href="#developer--nightly--experimental-installs-windows-powershell"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The developer install builds from the <code>main</code> branch, which is the latest (nightly) source.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth.git
cd unsloth
Set-ExecutionPolicy -Scope Process -ExecutionPolicy Bypass
.\install.ps1 --local
unsloth studio -p 8888"><pre>git clone https:<span class="pl-k">//</span><span class="pl-c1">github.com</span><span class="pl-k">/</span>unslothai<span class="pl-k">/</span>unsloth.git
cd unsloth
<span class="pl-c1">Set-ExecutionPolicy</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Scope <span class="pl-k">Process</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>ExecutionPolicy Bypass
.\install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">--</span>local
unsloth studio <span class="pl-k">-</span>p <span class="pl-c1">8888</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">To install into an isolated location (its own virtual env, <code>auth/</code>, <code>studio.db</code>, cache and llama.cpp build), set <code>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME</code> and pass it again at launch:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$env:UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME=&quot;$PWD\.studio&quot;; .\install.ps1 --local
$env:UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME=&quot;$PWD\.studio&quot;; unsloth studio -p 8888"><pre><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-c1">$PWD</span>\.studio<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>; .\install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">--</span>local
<span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-c1">$PWD</span>\.studio<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>; unsloth studio <span class="pl-k">-</span>p <span class="pl-c1">8888</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then to update :</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cd unsloth; git pull
.\install.ps1 --local
unsloth studio -p 8888"><pre>cd unsloth; git pull
.\install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">--</span>local
unsloth studio <span class="pl-k">-</span>p <span class="pl-c1">8888</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Remote access: <code>--secure</code> (HTTPS tunnel) vs raw port</h4><a id="user-content-remote-access---secure-https-tunnel-vs-raw-port" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Remote access: --secure (HTTPS tunnel) vs raw port" href="#remote-access---secure-https-tunnel-vs-raw-port"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default <code>unsloth studio</code> binds to <code>127.0.0.1</code> (this machine only). To reach it from another device, pick one of:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>--secure</code> (recommended): serve <strong>only</strong> through a free Cloudflare HTTPS link. Unsloth stays bound to localhost and the tunnel provides the public URL; it fails closed (does not start) if the tunnel can't come up, so the raw port is never exposed.</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="unsloth studio --secure -p 8888"><pre>unsloth studio --secure -p 8888</pre></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>-H 0.0.0.0</code>: bind the raw port on all network interfaces, reachable from anywhere on the network (subject to your firewall). It does not create a public internet URL; add <code>--cloudflare</code> to also publish an internet-reachable <code>https://*.trycloudflare.com</code> link even behind a firewall. Only use this on a network you trust.</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="unsloth studio -H 0.0.0.0 -p 8888"><pre>unsloth studio -H 0.0.0.0 -p 8888</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The Cloudflare tunnel is <strong>off by default</strong>: <code>-H 0.0.0.0</code> exposes the raw port only, not a public internet URL. Pair the wildcard bind with <code>--cloudflare</code> (<code>unsloth studio -H 0.0.0.0 --cloudflare</code>) to also publish a public <code>https://*.trycloudflare.com</code> link, or prefer <code>--secure</code> (above), which keeps the raw port private. <code>--cloudflare</code> has no effect on a loopback bind.</p>
<p dir="auto">On a wildcard bind Unsloth works out the address to share by asking <code>ifconfig.me</code> for the public IP, then asks <code>check-host.net</code> whether that port is reachable so it can tell you if a firewall is in the way. Both contact a third party. Set <code>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_DISABLE_PUBLIC_CHECK=1</code> to skip them; the banner then shows the LAN address and no reachability line.</p>
<p dir="auto">The first time Unsloth is published on a public URL (<code>--secure</code> or <code>--cloudflare</code>) with the auto-generated admin password still in place, it asks for a new admin password in the terminal (masked input with confirmation) before the public link goes up. Without an attached terminal it warns instead and keeps the bootstrap deadline: Unsloth shuts down after <code>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_BOOTSTRAP_TIMEOUT</code> (default 1 hour) unless the password is changed in the web UI.</p>
<p dir="auto">For headless setups that cannot answer that prompt, set the initial admin password non-interactively with <code>--password</code> (only takes effect when no password is set yet; if one already exists it is a hard error, so rotate later with <code>unsloth studio reset-password</code>):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="unsloth studio --secure --password 'your-strong-password'        # visible in `ps`/history
UNSLOTH_STUDIO_PASSWORD='your-strong-password' unsloth studio --secure   # via env var
printf '%s\n' 'your-strong-password' | unsloth studio --secure --password -   # via stdin"><pre>unsloth studio --secure --password <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>your-strong-password<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> visible in `ps`/history</span>
UNSLOTH_STUDIO_PASSWORD=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>your-strong-password<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> unsloth studio --secure   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> via env var</span>
<span class="pl-c1">printf</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>%s\n<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>your-strong-password<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> unsloth studio --secure --password -   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> via stdin</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">A literal <code>--password VALUE</code> is visible in the process list and shell history, so prefer the <code>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_PASSWORD</code> env var or <code>--password -</code> (stdin) for automation. This applies to any launch (public or a headless <code>-H 0.0.0.0</code> bind), and the password is set in the parent before the server binds, so it never reaches a re-executed child process.</p>
<p dir="auto">Server-side tools (web search, Python and terminal code execution) run as your user and are on by default. Anyone who can reach the server with the API key can run code on this machine, so keep your API key private and pass <code>--disable-tools</code> when exposing Unsloth.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Advanced launch options</h4><a id="user-content-advanced-launch-options" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Advanced launch options" href="#advanced-launch-options"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Installer options can be passed as environment variables. On macOS, Linux and WSL place the variable after the pipe so the shell passes it to <code>sh</code>; on Windows set it with <code>$env:</code> before piping to <code>iex</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Skip PyTorch (GGUF-only mode):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh | UNSLOTH_NO_TORCH=1 sh"><pre>curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> UNSLOTH_NO_TORCH=1 sh</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$env:UNSLOTH_NO_TORCH=1; irm https://unsloth.ai/install.ps1 | iex"><pre><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>UNSLOTH_NO_TORCH</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>; irm https:<span class="pl-k">//</span>unsloth.ai<span class="pl-k">/</span>install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">|</span> iex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Skip the post-install prompt that starts Unsloth (useful for automated installs):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh | UNSLOTH_SKIP_AUTOSTART=1 sh"><pre>curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> UNSLOTH_SKIP_AUTOSTART=1 sh</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$env:UNSLOTH_SKIP_AUTOSTART=1; irm https://unsloth.ai/install.ps1 | iex"><pre><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>UNSLOTH_SKIP_AUTOSTART</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>; irm https:<span class="pl-k">//</span>unsloth.ai<span class="pl-k">/</span>install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">|</span> iex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Pin the Python version:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh | UNSLOTH_PYTHON=3.12 sh"><pre>curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> UNSLOTH_PYTHON=3.12 sh</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$env:UNSLOTH_PYTHON='3.12'; irm https://unsloth.ai/install.ps1 | iex"><pre><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>UNSLOTH_PYTHON</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>3.12<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>; irm https:<span class="pl-k">//</span>unsloth.ai<span class="pl-k">/</span>install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">|</span> iex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Install to a custom location with <code>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh | UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME=/abs/path sh"><pre>curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME=/abs/path sh</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$env:UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME='C:\path'; irm https://unsloth.ai/install.ps1 | iex"><pre><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>UNSLOTH_STUDIO_HOME</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>C:\path<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>; irm https:<span class="pl-k">//</span>unsloth.ai<span class="pl-k">/</span>install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">|</span> iex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">On macOS, the installer defaults to the system certificate store (<code>UV_SYSTEM_CERTS=1</code>) so uv trusts the CAs in your Keychain, needed behind TLS-inspecting proxies (Cisco Umbrella, Zscaler, etc.). Opt out with:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh | UV_SYSTEM_CERTS=0 sh"><pre>curl -fsSL https://unsloth.ai/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> UV_SYSTEM_CERTS=0 sh</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Point the frontend build at a corporate npm mirror/proxy with <code>UNSLOTH_NPM_REGISTRY</code> (for the developer install behind a firewall that blocks <code>registry.npmjs.org</code>):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="UNSLOTH_NPM_REGISTRY=https://artifactory.example.com/api/npm/npm/ ./install.sh --local"><pre>UNSLOTH_NPM_REGISTRY=https://artifactory.example.com/api/npm/npm/ ./install.sh --local</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$env:UNSLOTH_NPM_REGISTRY='https://artifactory.example.com/api/npm/npm/'; .\install.ps1 --local"><pre><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>UNSLOTH_NPM_REGISTRY</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>https://artifactory.example.com/api/npm/npm/<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>; .\install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">--</span>local</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">It is threaded as <code>--registry</code> into the Unsloth frontend <code>npm</code>/<code>bun</code> installs; the supply-chain locks (7-day <code>min-release-age</code>, exact version pins) stay in force.</p>
<p dir="auto">Cap Unsloth's native CPU thread pools on high-core hosts: <code>UNSLOTH_CPU_THREADS=8 unsloth studio -p 8888</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Uninstall</h4><a id="user-content-uninstall" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Uninstall" href="#uninstall"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The recommended way to fully remove Unsloth Studio is the matching uninstall script for your OS. It stops any running servers, removes the install dir, the launcher data dir, the desktop shortcut, and any platform-specific entries (macOS <code>.app</code> bundle + Launch Services on Mac; Start Menu, <code>HKCU\Software\Unsloth</code> registry key and user <code>PATH</code> entries on Windows):</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>​ <strong>MacOS, WSL, Linux:</strong> <code>curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/unslothai/unsloth/main/scripts/uninstall.sh | sh</code></li>
<li>​ <strong>Windows (PowerShell):</strong> <code>irm https://raw.githubusercontent.com/unslothai/unsloth/main/scripts/uninstall.ps1 | iex</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">If you only want to drop the install dir and keep the launcher/shortcut for a later reinstall, you can instead run <code>rm -rf ~/.unsloth/studio</code> (Mac/Linux/WSL) or <code>Remove-Item -Recurse -Force "$HOME\.unsloth\studio"</code> (Windows). The model cache at <code>~/.cache/huggingface</code> is not touched by any of these.</p>
<p dir="auto">For more info, <a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/new/studio/install#uninstall" rel="nofollow">see our docs</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Deleting model files</h4><a id="user-content-deleting-model-files" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deleting model files" href="#deleting-model-files"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can delete old model files either from the bin icon in model search or by removing the relevant cached model folder from the default Hugging Face cache directory. By default, HF uses:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>​ <strong>MacOS, Linux, WSL:</strong> <code>~/.cache/huggingface/hub/</code></li>
<li>​ <strong>Windows:</strong> <code>%USERPROFILE%\.cache\huggingface\hub\</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">💚 Community and Links</h2><a id="user-content--community-and-links" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 💚 Community and Links" href="#-community-and-links"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
